## Artifact sets (v351)
Simon asked for "artifact synergies": three equipped artifacts of one element (all three sockets) give that element's scrolls a set bonus. In the game they are called **artifact sets** (scroll combos are already called synergies). `_backup/as_b.js`, inserted by `_backup/p_as.py`, which also routes `haloMat` through `HALO_MK` / `HALO_FX`. Like every artifact since v325, sets act only on the hero's own scrolls (`o.spell`), not on towers or the hero's plain attacks.
- **Lightning, Crimson Storm:** lightning scroll hits x1.25; a hit leaves the foe Shocked (`e.shockT` 5 s); the next lightning scroll hit consumes it for a blast of 45% of that hit to the foe and every foe within 1 tile (`asShockBlast`).
- **Fire, Blackfire:** fire scroll blasts through `splashAt` reach 15% further (and their rings, shockwaves and decals are drawn 15% wider); every fire hit scorches the ground (`AS.bg`, 4 s, radius 0.575, max 40): ground foes on it burn for 15% of the hero's power a second, flyers above it wither.
- **Ice, Deep Winter:** a foe frozen by a set ice scroll (`stunE` inside the context, or a stun straight after a set ice hit, as Eternal Winter does) has `e.frzT`; other foes within 1.1 walk 50% slower, and 1.2 s in that chill brings Heavy Frost (1 s stun set directly, so it ignores Resolve; once per foe, `e.hfDone`). Frost Tower freezes never count.
- **Dark, Grave Runes:** dark scroll hits leave mist (`AS.mist`, 3 s, wither 12% of the hero's power a second); dark kills (killing blow from a dark scroll, `e.lastEl`) drop a rune 33% of the time (max 10, 25 s): Wither, Poison (`e.asPsn`), Dark Blast (150% in 1.2), Dark Lightning (120% plus two arcs at 72%) or Bound (0.5 s stun ignoring Resolve), taken by the next ground foe to step on it.
- **Light, Pale Verdict:** light scroll hits mark the foe (`e.lmT` 6 s); a marked foe's death rolls 5% Piercing Judgement (500% of the hero's power, width 0.6, Weakens) or a further 10% Piercing Verdict (200%, width 0.35), a beam through the corpse from a random angle; 1% of light hits (at most one roll per foe every 0.4 s) are a Blood Pact: red pillar, bleeding (`e.asBld`, 3% max HP a second, 1% on bosses), a drain of 10% max HP (3% on bosses) and, below 30% health, a kill (never a boss).

**Looks.** `AS.ctx` is on while a set scroll's code runs: `castScrollNow`, plus the `addFx` callbacks, `setTimeout` timers and `shoot` hits it creates, plus `statusTick` for foes burning from the fire set or Charged under the lightning set. In the context, three.js classes are subclassed (materials, meshes, lines, points, sprites, point lights, `THREE.Color`): new materials and colours in the element's range are remapped in place by `asMapRGB` (lightning to dark red, purple to dark purple-black; fire to dark red-black; ice to dark blue or white; light to pale grey), `ShaderMaterial`s get the same remap injected as GLSL (`AS_GL`), and shared materials on new meshes (`NS_ICE`, `M()`, `haloMat`) are swapped for recoloured clones cached in `AS.sw`, so the caches stay clean. Additive glows turn normal (dark colours would vanish); for light only the solid shapes do. Particles (`emit`, `emitS`) are remapped and sent to the normal systems. The remap is idempotent (its outputs fall outside its input range), so `M()`/`haloMat` keys and `Color.copy`/`lerp` on recoloured colours (`c.asOwn`) never compound. Materials made in the context are registered (WeakRef) and put back when a battle runs with a different set (`asRestore`). Dark keeps its colours, so it never enters the context.

**UI.** Heroes > Artifacts shows an "Artifact sets" box under the chamber (`asBoxHtml`, five rows with pips, the active one open); the in-game artifact card adds a "Set bonus" row; `#artHud` glows in the set's colour; a toast names the set when a battle starts.

**Tests.** `tests/sets-test.js` (in run-all): the remap, set detection, every mechanic and its rate, the screens, cache cleanliness and restore, then the sweep: every lightning, fire, ice and light scroll cast with its set on, counting any material, mesh or particle in the set's colour range made outside the context (`AS.dbg`; skips terrain ripples, the Resolve break puff and gem-carrier sparkles). `window.__asOnly='mech'` or an element runs one part.

## Dimensional Slash (v352)
Simon asked for a new scroll inspired by Black Clover, Yami's Dimension Slash, balanced as I saw fit and as cool as possible. `_backup/dslash_b.js`, inserted before the boot line by `_backup/p_dslash.py` (args: src, module, version; refuses to run without v351's `const AS={el:null`).
- **The scroll:** key `dslash`, legendary, dark, cd 45, `ANIM_D` 2.6, `CAST_REL` 2.3 (the hero stays in the cast through the cut), in `TSP` (Aevorian's space bonus). Numbers in `DSL`.
- **Aim:** `dslAim` picks a ray from the hero to the board edge (`dslLen`) through the most foes (bosses count 3), from candidates towards each foe plus 72 directions, then a fine sweep; it re-aims at T 0.8. A foe is on the line within 0.5 tiles (`dslOnLine`).
- **Timeline:** 0-0.85 the draw (a katana of dark magic, `dslBladeGeo`, placed in world space at the hero's right hand, `rig.W.o` hidden meanwhile; dark smoke aura); 0.85 one sweep (crescent shader in the cut's frame) with a hit-stop (`worldSlow` .08); a held beat; 1.4 the split: `dslInvert` flips the canvas to a negative for 95 ms (skipped with Calm space-bending), shields are stripped, 1200% to every foe on the line, `jdgFlashWhite` and a white slice mark on each, invisible foes revealed; the rift until 7.4 (45% slow, 45% of the power every 0.25 s as dot); it seals from both ends by 8.1.
- **Warp:** one `SDP.sl` slash slot (`sdpInit()` first, `SDP.n++` at the sweep); the slot object is re-claimed every frame because the Citadel nulls all four slots when it ends. Amplitude is scaled by `SPACE_K`.
- **Sound:** `dslSound` (gather, draw, split, rift drone, seal) on `SFX.master`.
- **Power:** walk metric (0.4 one + 0.6 pack/3, per second of cooldown) about 56 averaged over 5 plains seeds, level with Voidfall (55); 32-78 by road layout.
- **Tests:** `tests/dslash-test.js` (35, in run-all); sheets with `tests/dslash-shots.js` (`__ds.sheet(name,[t],{arena,seed,close})`, drawn through `sdpRender`).

## Secret scrolls and the events
Rarity id `unique` is shown as **Secret**. Secret scrolls carry `ev` ('Blood Moon' or 'Starfall') and only drop from Scroll Bearers during that event.
Which one a bearer carries is weighted by power in `EVW` (`evPick(ev)`, `evPct(k)` for the Spellbook): Blood Moon = Blood Needles 40, Eye of the
Blood Moon 32, Moonfall 17, Curse 11; Starfall = Constellation 38, Pulsar 26, Wormhole 24, Singularity 12. The Spellbook sorts secrets last and
hides their origin, type and description until owned. Batch 4 (search `Secret scrolls batch 4`) holds the four newer ones; marked foes from the Eye
take +35% damage (`e.eyeT` against game `time`, a `hurt` wrapper). Watch for name clashes there: the Curse already owns `bmBolt` and a local
`segDist`, so batch 4 uses `bm2Bolt` and `nsSegDist`.

The Blood Moon look (search `Blood Moon, made grander`) wraps `bmAnnounce` (heartbeats, `bmWave` tide, blood lightning, crows) and `bmAtmos`
(crimson sky lerp, `bmGround` blood-mist floor shader with heartbeat veins, embers, ash, fog, distant lightning, crows). The CSS moon, eclipse and
rays are styled there too.

## Wandering merchant
Search `Wandering merchant` (the block right after the scroll art is built). After a cleared wave (from wave `WM_FROM` = 3, at least `WM_GAP` = 4 waves
after his last visit) there is a flat 1% chance (`WM_CH` = `WM_CAP` = .01 since v303; 0.5% from v284 to v302, 15% rising 3% a wave before 2026-10-20) that a wagon rolls in through the nearest gate (`wmGates`,
grid BFS + string pulling, Catmull-Rom curve), parks on a free 3x3 patch and sets up a stall. Tapping the patch (`wmPick`, called from `tap()`) opens
the shop: 4 scrolls, rarity rolled per slot with `WM_RW`, unowned scrolls first, priced in gems by `WM_PRICE`; buying one you own adds +1 level. He
stays `WM_STAY` = 3 cleared waves or until sold out, then packs up and drives back out (no route: he vanishes in smoke).
* State is `WM`; it is saved with the run as `serializeRun().wm` (old saves without it load fine). His 9 tiles are grid 2 while he is there.
* Everything he adds to the scene sits under `WM.root`; `wmDrop()` removes it (wrapped into `resetRun` and `buildMap`).
* The developer menu can bring him at once or send him away (`WM.force` still makes him come after the next cleared wave).
* Tests: `tests/merchant-test.js` (`__wm.checks()` for the logic, `await __wm.sheet(name,[t...],{focus})` for frame sheets).

## Developer mode (testing only)
The More tab's Testing tools button switches `meta.dev` (saved per account). Switching it on copies the account's progress (`DEV_KEYS`) to
`meta.devSnap`, unlocks every scroll, hero and tower at level 1, and pins gold, gems, crowns and scraps at `DEV_INF` (the top bars show an infinity
sign). Switching it off puts `meta.devSnap` back, and a run in progress drops to `startGold` for its wave. While it is on, every run shows a wrench button pinned under the pause
button (`#bDev`, placed by `devSync` from `#bQPause`'s position and size) that opens `#devPanel`: start or end the Blood Moon (`BM.until`) and the
Starfall (`SF.until`, the two end each other), bring or send away the merchant, put an Ill Omen on the next wave (`DEV.omen`, read once by
`buildWave`, then `nextQ` is rebuilt), spawn a Scroll Bearer of any rarity (`forceScroll`/`forceScrollR`) or a Gem Carrier (`forceGem`), add
lives, defeat every foe, recharge every cooldown, and unlock the Cookbook, Bestiary and start waves (`devGiveAll`, the old Give me everything code).
Everything lives in the `TESTING ONLY` block plus the one `DEV.omen` check in `buildWave`, so removing it before release is one cut.
Since v294 developer mode also opens every land the game can unlock (`devLands` in the tundra module, called from `devPin` and a `migrateMeta`
wrapper; lands marked `soon` stay shut). Only `meta.arenas` changes: the real lands are in `meta.devSnap` and come back when it is turned off (the board
is rebuilt if the keep stood in a land the real account has not opened). Unvisited lands still play their lock break on the first trip.
Tests: `tests/dev-test.js` (returns [name, ok, detail] rows).

Nothing is shared with Kingsgaard at build or run time. `../_pre-separation/` holds the untouched combined game.

## Blood rain, tundra sea, Infinite Void (v354)
`_backup/v354_b.js`, inserted by `_backup/p_v354.py`, on top of v353.
- **Blood rain in the world.** `BR3`: 1100 slim red streaks (one `InstancedMesh`, boxes 0.022 wide, 0.42 to 0.72 long) fall at 10 to 13.5 a second with the wind (-1.3, 0.45) over the board plus a margin (`br3Box`). Each drop knows where it will land (`gy` at the landing point, worked out at spawn), and 10% of landings splash: a growing ring from a pool of 64 plus two droplets. Driven from `bmAtmos` (the frame loop, real time; frozen while paused), fading with `BM.k`. The 2D screen rain is now a faint near layer (`bmRainDraw` at 30%), and the old random splashes are off (`BMRN.splK=0`, patched into the v337 line).
- **Tundra sea.** A `tunGround` wrapper rewrites the sea part of the around-the-board shader at compile time (`TUN_SEA_OLD` to `TUN_SEA_NEW`; it logs an error and keeps the old sea if the text ever changes): turquoise shallows into deep blue, two layers of drifting ripples, caustics in the shallows, surf bands rolling in, lacy foam at the waterline, warm glints on wave crests (added after lighting, at `dithering_fragment`), and a foam ring with a pale glow around each iceberg. `TUNB.uBerg` (7 circles) is read from the berg `InstancedMesh` after `buildMap`, so no random numbers change and saved boards stay the same. Program key `kktunautumn4a`.
- **Infinite Void.** Its `collect` now skips only `userData.voidKeep` (the castle, gates, chests, road pebbles, the tundra and Whitewaste hover blob and the tundra build grid) and dissolves instanced meshes too; their reach for the hide rule comes from their instances (`voidInstFar`). Trees, every prop and everything outside the board dissolve; towers, the hero and foes are not terrain and stay.
Tests: `tests/v354-test.js` (27, in run-all).

## Nature, the sixth element (v355)

- **Scrolls** (each registered in SPELLS_S, SCROLL_IDS, SPELL_EL, SPELL_FX, ANIM_D, POSES, SIG, SPI and NSP): Thorn Whip (common, 150%), Bramble Patch (uncommon, 6 s zone, 45%/s, 35% slow), Entangling Roots (rare, up to 5 foes, 170% and held 1.2 s), Oakheart Treant (epic, a modelled treant walking up the road for 10 s, 240% slams every 1.3 s), Hundred-Year Bloom (legendary, a giant flower opens for 1.6 s holding foes, bursts for 700%, pollen 5 s), Wrath of Gaia (mythic, trees erupt along every road for 500% and a hold, then 300% to every foe and the keep mends 2). Vines are `TubeGeometry` grown through the draw range (`natVine`); leaves and petals are small fluttering meshes (`natLeaves`); the kit is shared (`natKit`).

Each has `ART_G` and `ART_LVD`.
- **Evergreen set** (`AS_D.nature`): +20% nature scroll damage, every hit Overgrown, Overgrown deaths leave a thicket, every 30th nature kill mends the keep. No colour change.
- **Synergies**: Brushfire, Frostbloom, Grounded, Sunbloom, Rotbloom, Deep Roots (the last two not on Ancient scrolls), Warden Oak (the Treant roots Overgrown foes instead of knocking them back), Pollen Blast, World Root (Gaia while `ANC.live.firsttree`). Element reactions sit in a `comboReact` wrapper.

## Arena 5: the Deep Taiga (v356)
`_backup/tg_b.js` + `tgboss_b.js` + `tgmarks_b.js`, inserted together by `_backup/p_taiga.py` (args: src html, tg_b.js, version string for the CLOG entry) before the boot line, on top of v355 (Nature). Simon: "add arena 5 the Deep taiga with everything unique it needs and tougher enemies / available after clearing arena 4".
- **The land.** The map site `taiga` (already painted) becomes playable: `ARENAS.taiga` (no 5), `SITE_ARENA`, `arenaNow`. It opens with a wave-200 win in the Whitewaste (`arenaWin` wrapper; `arenaFix` opens it for accounts with `wonA.whitewaste`); the lock text, arrival plate, `arenaUnlockDev`, header class and the Bestiary biome cycle (Plains > Frostvale > Tundra > Whitewaste > Taiga) all know it. `wmpVia` is wrapped with a road search (breadth first over `WMP.paths`) so a trip from the tundra or the Whitewaste runs along the roads too.
- **Tougher.** `TG`: troops x1.3 health and x1.05 speed, bosses x1.5 health (spawnEnemy, and `bossHpAt` agrees); three gates (`gateN`); gems x2 and rarity +16% (`ARENA_REWARD.taiga`); fire spells +15% (`LAND_EL.taiga={ice:1,slow:1,fire:1.15}` plus its own `hurt` wrapper; the ice wrapper multiplies by `ice`, so it must stay 1). Peat bogs: `terMarshLand` includes the taiga (the plains marsh, dressed from `TERP.taiga`: dark peat water, cotton grass).
- **Wolves.** `EN.wolf` (hp 30, spd 1.7, armour .05, `one:'Wolf'` for its singular name: `unitName` and the Bestiary title use `EN[t].one` when set): a troop of its own (`tgWolfModel`, four leg pivots driven by `tick`, no `limbs`), in the Bestiary (`GAL_UNITS`, `ENEMY_VARIANTS`), gem carriers wear the gem on a collar (`gemWeapon` wrapper). A `buildWave` wrapper turns 35% of peasants and riders into wolves from wave 8 in the taiga only (never elite: elite gear assumes a body).
- **The pack (`TGP`).** Every 30-42 s with a wave on the field: `packSpot` picks a mid-road point and the nearest board edge; 2.2 s of warning (`sfxHowl`, the `#tgPill` sign, glowing eyes at board height among the first spruce, an amber ring on the road), then `packAt` drops `packCount()` wolves (3 + wave/35, up to 7; 80% health, half gold) around the spot with `ox/oz` offsets from the treeline, so they dash in (moveEnemy decays the offset). From wave 50 the first one is an alpha (x3 health, x1.35 size, dark with a pale mane). `tgCalm(n)` keeps the pack away n waves, `TGP.bait` doubles its rate; both saved with the run (`tgp`). Developer menu row 'Wolf pack' (`packNow`).
- **Board (`taigaBoard`).** Moss tiles 0x30503a, a road of earth and needles 0x8a7760 (lum about .26 vs .48), `tgGround` shader (two mosses at the same brightness, shade patches, specks; needles and ruts on the road), `TG_REMAP` for spruce/trunks/tufts/sides/lip, `tgMoss` on rocks (board rocks keep their colour). Outside the grid, instanced: about 330 tall spruce (`tgSpruceGeo`, five tiers, never in front of the board), birches, fallen logs, mossy boulders, ferns, toadstools, stumps, road-edge litter, rare inner ferns hidden under new towers (`TGD.deco`), 14 drifting mist planes. `TG_LIGHT` (night brightened so the board stays readable), `TG_PV`. `tgAtmos`: needles by day, fireflies at night.
- **Foes' look.** `tgify`/`tgMat`: a little earthier and darker with a warm rim light; `tgGear`: a fur collar (`tgFurGeo`) and a string of fangs (`tgFangGeo`).
- **Bosses (`tgboss_b.js`).** `MSB_AT.taiga={100:['mossback',14000],200:['leshy',36000]}`. Mossback (`mossbackModel`, rear/roar/curl/sleep in `g.userData`): maul every 9 s (6.5 enraged) seizes 2 towers in 2.3 tiles for 2 s after a 0.5 s rear-up; roar every 13 s gives foes within 3 tiles `tgRoar` 3 s (+35% speed); at half health sleeps 3.5 s (`mbSleep`: stands still, takes 40% damage, heals 12%), then +20% speed. The Leshy (`leshyModel`): roots (`tgSeize(t,2.5,'root')`) on 3 towers in 3 tiles every 10 s, calls the pack (`tgCallPack`) every 15 s, at half health a bark shield (+18%), 3 s of mist (`burrow`, untargetable) and another pack; its death calls `tgCalm(3)`. `tgSeize` uses the frozen-tower mechanism (`iceT`, `iceM`); the `msbThaw` wrapper releases its own looks. Gotcha: boss abilities run inside a `moveEnemy` wrapper only when dt>0, so a boss that must stand still keeps dt and gets its `dist` put back (never call moveEnemy with 0 for that).
- **Places (`tgmarks_b.js`).** Lore props elk skull, bear trap, carved idol, birch-bark canoe, store hut on a post, ring of mossy stones. Landmarks: Old Stockade (archers, wolves x2), Hunting Stand (no pack within 4 tiles, wolves +25% damage within 3), Spirit Totem (towers within 3 tiles +10% damage), Old Sawmill (salvage, or +40+3w gold after each cleared wave), Hut on Hen's Legs (+3 lives, or a curse: +15% damage taken within 3 tiles), the Old Grove (frozen/rooted/clawed towers within 3 tiles recover twice as fast, or fell it: gold, 5 gems, foes +10% speed for 3 waves). Frostvale already owns 'lodge' and 'den', and LM_DONE is shared across lands, so every state name is new. Chest: `MK_CHEST.taiga` plus moss and antlers (the tundra's dressing skips the taiga).
Nature resistances: wolf 1.2, Mossback .8, the Leshy .4.
Tests: `tests/taiga-test.js` (73, in run-all); `tests/v354-test.js` also checks the Infinite Void in the taiga; `tests/boss-test.js` expects six Bestiary pages; `tests/arena-test.js` cycles five biomes. Shots: `_backup/tg_shot.js` (`__tgRun`, `__tgTime`, `__spotShot`).

## Grave Runes turn every scroll purple (v356)
`_backup/asdark_b.js`, inserted by `_backup/p_asdark.py` (args: src html, module, version) before the boot line, on top of v355 + `p_taiga.py`. Simon: "upgrade the dark synergy from artifacts that all spells are purple".
- **Every scroll.** With the dark set on, the `castScrollNow` wrapper runs every hero scroll in the set context (`AS.el==='dark'||SPELL_EL[k]===AS.el`); the other sets still colour only their own element and the nature set keeps its colours (`asLive` excludes only nature now). `asHex`/`asColor` no longer skip dark.
- **The dark remap.** `asMapRGB` dark branch and `AS_GL.dark`: any colour with saturation over .12 that is not purple yet (hue outside .69-.88) moves to hue .72-.82 (elements still differ a little), saturation .5+.45s, brightness kept (highlights x.92); near-white (s<=.12, L>.62) turns lavender (hue .77, s .72, L up to .8); greys, smoke and black stay. Purple input is untouched, so it is idempotent. The GLSL keeps HDR intensity (x max channel). Purple glows stay additive (`asPaint`/`asShader` skip the normal-blend switch for dark).
- **Statuses.** A hero-scroll hit stamps `e.adkT`; for 10 s the foe's whole `statusTick` runs in the context (an outermost wrapper, because nature's Overgrown wrapper sits outside the set's own), plus the inner `on` check for hero burns.
- **Textures and vertex colours.** `adkTexHook` (an `asMat` wrapper): a material made in the context with a `map` or vertex colours gets `asMap` injected after `color_fragment` (or `map_fragment`) through `onBeforeCompile` (cache key `|adk`); the `asRestore` wrapper takes the hook off when the set changes.
- Mechanics unchanged: mist and runes still come only from dark hits (`asHit`/`asKill` key on the hit's element). Towers are never recoloured.
Tests: `tests/asdark-test.js` (18, in run-all: remap, idempotence, context, other sets unchanged, additive glows, texture hook and restore, a sweep of two scrolls per other element with `AS.dbg` leak count 0). Shots: `tests/asdark-shots.js` (`__adk.sheet(name,[ids],t)`: each scroll without a set and with the dark set).

## Piercing Judgment and Piercing Verdict, smoother (v357)
Simon: "make piercing verdict and judgement more cool and smooth". Looks only. `_backup/verdict_b.js`, inserted before the boot line by `_backup/p_verdict.py` (args: src, module, version; refuses to run without v356's `AS_GL.dark=`). It reassigns `castJudgment=jvCast` and `asBeam=jvAsBeam`; the old functions stay in `JV.old` for the parity test.
- **Unchanged:** the aim (`jdgAim`, the re-aim chance of 6 per second, the final re-aim at the shot), the hit line (from the circle point of the shot frame's aim, widths .48/.34, +.25 for bosses), 700% / 375%, the Judgment's execute under 15%, the timings (FORM/HOLD/COMP/FIRE .55/.85/1.15/1.2 and .32/.36/.56/.6), `ANIM_D`, `CAST_REL`, cooldowns.
- **Circle:** each layer is a `ShaderMaterial` (`jvSigMat`, `JV_SFS`) traced in by a pen: ink shows behind a sweep front (`uRev`), the front glows (`uTip`) and throws sparks; it eases in (no pop), spins up continuously, and collapses with the outer layer first (`uHot` brightens it). The circle turns to a new aim smoothly (`lerpAng`, 12 per second, like the hero); the shot still uses the unsmoothed point. Motes spiral in; a ring implodes; a lens streak and a pooled ground glow.
- **Light:** the shared `FXL` as a palm light (`jvLight`/`jvLightOff`): taken only while it is dark or ours, handed back at 0. Peak about .6 at the shot; more washes the plains out to white.
- **Beam:** a soft ribbon turned to the camera about its own axis (`jvRibbon`, `jvLay`, `JV_RFS`: white core, coloured glow, halo, hot tip, shimmer). It shoots out in .06/.045 s, thins to a hairline, dissolves from the hand outward (`uA`) by about .95/.62 s after the shot. Muzzle rings, a seared line that glows then cools, motes rising off the line.
- **Hits:** damage lands at the shot as before; the white flash (`jvFlash`, body only: transparent, additive and depth-write-off parts such as the blob shadow, aura rings and shield bubble are hidden) and the exit sparks and puncture (`jvHole`, Judgment) follow the tip.
- **Poses:** `POSES.judgment`/`verdict` gain a small brace as the circle collapses and a sharper recoil with a slower settle.
- **Pale Verdict set:** `jvAsBeam` opens the ribbon out both ways from the fallen foe, then thins and fades (same lives .95/.65).
- **No stutter:** every material of the cast is drawn (unseen) from its first frame, so nothing compiles at the shot; both shaders are also compiled at load into the real scene (`JV.warm` keeps the programs alive; disposing them would free the program). Created inside the cast, so the set looks (light, dark since v356) recolour them.
- **Tests:** `tests/verdict-test.js` (21, in run-all): old vs new on the same foes (same foes hit, same damage, same executes, nothing before the shot), walking crowds in five layouts with 0.17 s steps (the re-aim then happens every frame, so both are deterministic: identical), effects tracked by call stack all gone, no shader program added after the second frame, the light handed back, the poses, the set beam. Sheets with `tests/verdict-shots.js` (`__vd.sheet(name,'judgment'|'verdict',[t],{close,dx,dz,crop,cols,set})`; `set` equips three artifacts of an element with developer mode and restores them).

## The nature scrolls, in full bloom (v358)
Simon: "can you visually enhance all nature spells please". Looks only: damage, holds, zones and timings are v355's (nature-test still 47/47). `_backup/natfx_b.js`, inserted before the boot line by `_backup/p_natfx.py` (args: src, module; refuses to run without v355's `castThornWhip` or twice), on top of v357. It reassigns `natVine`, `natTreantModel`, `natTree` and the six `cast*` functions; the v355 helpers (`natKit`, `natDirt`, `natMend`, `ovgApply`) stay.
- **Kit (`nfxKit`, `NFX.k`).** Shared vertex-colour Phong (`vc`, `vcS`), rose, berry, gold, birch, pine, blossom and lily materials; a cupped pointed petal (`petalG`, deep pink base to white tip in vertex colours) and a broad leaf (`bigLeafG`). `nfxRemapVC` runs vertex colours through `asMapRGB` while a set colours the cast (the dark set), so clones are recoloured and the shared geometry never is. The kit (and the crack materials, `NFX.cm`) is built inside `asOffRun`: a set recolours every colour made in its context, so a first cast under the dark set would otherwise bake purple into the shared petals for good.
- **Vines (`natVine`).** Tapered tubes (root to tip, `opt.taper`) with dark-to-bright vertex colours from a palette ('vine', 'root', 'bramble'; the old material argument still picks one), thorns, leaves, wild roses and berries that pop in as `grow(k)` passes them, an optional glowing tip. The parts are one `InstancedMesh` per shape and colour on each vine (`slot`, zero matrices until they appear); the tube's `dispose` frees them.
- **Ground.** `nfxRune` (a green druid circle, `kk` dims it), `nfxBurst` (cracks, a heaving dirt mound that `mound:0` turns off, clods, stones), `nfxLeaves` (drifting leaves or petals). Cracks are `nfxCracks`: two merged meshes (dark and glow, `NFX_BOXF` boxes) whose segments stretch like `crackSet`, instead of six meshes a crack.
- **Scrolls.** Thorn Whip: a burst and circle at the base. Bramble: 16 canes with roses and berries, ivy, moss, a wilt leaf fall. Roots: a circle under every other target (runes are 13 meshes each), a burst, 5 roots with rootlets. Treant: new model (trunk sections, ridges, knots, glowing eyes and halo, brow, mossy beard, 8 crown clusters with blossoms, two-part arms with fingers, root toes; returns `{g,body,arms,legs,geos,eyes}`), rises through a circle and burst, footstep dust, leaf drift, slams with root spikes and cracks, sinks at the end. Bloom: three rings of 25 petals opening in stages on 7 broad leaves, 18 stamens (instanced), a glow, vines to the foes it holds, a pillar of light, flying petals and `nfxPollenHaze`. Gaia: a circle and pillar at the keep, shockwaves, a burst and moss under every tree, leaves and blossom petals shed at the end.
- **Gaia trees (`natTree`).** Four kinds (pine 40%, oak 35%, birch 15%, blossom 10%; `userData.kind`), built by `nfxTreeRaw` outside the set context and merged into one vertex-coloured mesh (`nfxMergeTree`, `userData.geo`), recoloured once.
- **Cleanup.** `updFx` runs an effect's `end` only while `state==='playing'` and `clearWorld` removes only each effect's `o`, so the Bloom's vines (under `hb`) and Gaia's trees (under `root`) hang off their effect and their geometries sit in its `geos`. Before v358, Gaia's trees could stay on the board after a run ended mid-cast.
- **Cost.** Draw calls added at the peak: Thorn Whip 17, Bramble 112, Roots 141, Treant 117, Bloom 85, Gaia 84 (v357: 9/100/100/53/36/51; the first unbatched draft was 47/353/391/217/216/439). The board itself is about 219.
Tests: `tests/natfx-test.js` (30, in run-all): the kit (also when first built in a dark-set cast), tapering and colours, parts popping in and batched, every scroll drawing and cleaning up (what it added to the scene and looks like nature, kit materials, vertex colours or instanced parts, is gone; every vine geometry freed), a board cleared mid-cast leaving nothing, the draw-call budget (170; a hidden pane draws nothing, so it passes trivially there), 25 petals on 7 leaves, the Treant's face, four kinds of tree each one mesh, the dark set recolouring petals and vines.

## The Primordial Tempest and the Gathering Storm (v359)
Simon asked for "a spell like Vael Thorunn but ancient": an ancient lightning domain with Vael'Thoruun's purple lightning and precise strikes, plus something that enhances it. `_backup/tempest_b.js`, inserted before the boot line by `_backup/p_tempest.py` (args: src, module, version; refuses to run before v355). It also patches `heroCasting` so `ANC.lift` keeps the floating hero casting, and adds a notes section ('bosses-1') and a CLOG entry.
- **The scroll:** key `tempest`, ancient, lightning, cd 160, global, `ANIM_D` 19.8, ignores resistances like every ancient (`o.anc`). Built on the ancient domain frame (`ancBegin`, `ancIntro` with its OLDER THAN TIME card, `ancPool`, `ancView`, the `ANC.L` light; its light colours are made inside `asOffRun`, so an artifact set never blacks out the board) and Vael'Thoruun's bolts (`vBolt`, `vImpact`). `tpRun` is `ancRun` plus `extend(s,hi)`: the frame gets `Te = max(S0, T - added)` once the strike phase starts, so the storm can be held longer at any point.
- **Timeline (domain time):** 0-2.4 the storm gathers (violet light, storm-cloud shadows spiralling round the hero with a clear eye, rain, the sigil `tpTex('sig')`/`tpTex('star')` drawn round by `tpSigMat`); 2.4 the crown bolt strikes the hero and lifts it 1.55 (`ANC.lift`, a `heroUpdate` wrapper; a light pillar under it, purple smoke aura), and a ring rolls out at 24 tiles/s: 200% to every foe, Charged, invisible foes seen; 3.4-15.1 volleys of precise strikes; 15.6 the last thunder locks every foe (reticles for the first 40), struck 16.4-16.9 in a ripple out from the hero for 1000%; 16.9 the hero comes down on one great bolt (300% within 2.6) and is free at 17.4; light back by 19.2. Every foe is 25% slower (`ANC.fs.tempest`) while it rages.
- **Precise strikes:** `volley()` every 1.15 s easing to 0.75 s, 2 rising to 7 foes, ranked bosses, elites, then the healthiest, then the closest to the keep; a foe struck in the last 1.1 s waits, a boss can take two. `lockOn` gives each a reticle (`tpTex('ret')`, closing and spinning), a beam from the clouds that narrows onto it, and a leader line; after 0.5 s (+0.06 s per place in the volley) `fire` drops a vBolt from 7.5 up onto the foe's current position (re-aimed at the nearest unmarked foe if it already fell). `hit`: 450% + 0.4% max HP (0.15% bosses), Charged 4 s, `stunE` 0.3 s (bosses 0.12, Resolve applies), reveal, and an arc to the nearest charged foe within 2.2 for 35%. `TPST.dbg` (an array) logs every strike for tests.
- **The Gathering Storm (synergy `gatherstorm`, in the Cookbook):** a `comboPrep` wrapper: a lightning scroll cast while the storm rages extends it 1.5 s (up to 4.5 s, `TPST.ext`) and drops a bolt into the hero (`TPST.feed`). A `comboReact` wrapper: a lightning hit from another hero scroll (`o.spell`, not `o.art`) marks its foe for a called precise bolt, 200%, once per foe every 2 s, at most 8 waiting (`TPST.call`). A `synergyOpen` wrapper lets lightning scrolls through another great spell's casting lock while the storm rages.
- **Why a synergy:** every player who owns the scroll can trigger it with any lightning scroll (no 100-wave artifact drop), it leaves the artifact counts, drops and sets alone, and it is how the game already ties scrolls together, including the ancients (World Root, Void Light, Endless Dark). Vael'Thoruun cast inside the storm is the showcase.
- **Calm space-bending:** the storm keeps `SPACE_T` up, so with the setting on its flashes, light flicker and shake ease to `SPACE_K` 0.15.
- **Power:** walk-style at P 100, foes of 20000 health at speed 0.9, unpaused, 22 s: 43k pack of 8 / 7.1k one foe (270 per second of cooldown). Abyssal Genesis 54-60k / 6.9-8k, the Eldritch Tree 26k / 3.3k, Infinite Void 64k / kills one, the Aeon Dial (mythic) 29k / 3.2k. Note that `__st.walk` reads ancient domains as 0, because it pauses the game and domains only run while playing.
- **Tests:** `tests/tempest-test.js` (43, in run-all); frame sheets with `tests/tempest-shots.js` (`__tps.sheet(name,[t],{close,focus:'boss'|'hero',follow,set,calm,feed})`; the domain starts about 1 s after `fireScroll`, so sheet time = domain time + 1).

## Dimensional Slash: body-only hit flash (v360)
Simon accepted the follow-up from v357: Dimensional Slash's split flashed whole foes white, so their blob shadows, elite aura rings and gem rings showed as white discs. `_backup/dslflash_b.js`, inserted before the boot line by `_backup/p_dslflash.py` (args: src, module, version; needs v357's `jvFlash`).
- `jdgFlashWhite=jvFlash` for every caller (the old one is in `JV.oldFlash`). The callers are Dimensional Slash's split and the pre-v357 Piercing Judgment in `JV.old` (test-only). Looks only: damage and timing unchanged.
- **Tests:** `tests/verdict-test.js` checks an elite's flash shows no transparent, additive or depth-write-off part and keeps the body (skipped on builds without `JV.oldFlash`). `tests/dslash-test.js` is unchanged and passes. Sheets: the before/after split frames were drawn with `tests/dslash-shots.js`, the camera centred on the foes the split hits.

## Never-Never Land (v361)
`_backup/never_b.js`, inserted by `_backup/p_never.py` (args: src html, module, version for the CLOG entry) before the boot line, on top of v360 (built and tested on v357-v360; it also runs side by side with the Primordial Tempest, v359). Simon: "make spell from black clover called never neverland, it was used by Yuno ... fitting rarity, nice stats ... so we can see the stars and everything inside". Yuno's Star x Wind spirit magic with Sylph (manga ch. 356): a dome where he controls the flow of magic and time, boosting allies, weakening foes, time standing still.
- **The scroll.** Key `never`, ancient, light, `Domain / the starry sky`, cd 160, global; `ANIM_D` 21.5 with its own pose (arms raised as the dome opens, one hand guiding the stars, both sweeping down at the finale); `SIG`/`SPI` a star under a dome with wind; `ANC_EP.never` 'The sky where time does not move'. Built on the ancient kit (`ancBegin`/`ancRun`/`ancIntro`/`ancPool`/`ancView`), so it gets the OLDER THAN TIME card, the time stop and the parchment, and ignores resistances.
- **Timeline (`NVR`).** 0.1 s Sylph is born over the hero (a burst of starlight); 0.9-3 s the dome unfolds from her over the view (`ancView`, radius 7-15) with a wave of starlight, the night falls (`C.L`: hemi .3, sun .2, indigo sky), the stars ignite as the dome front passes them; `ACT0` 3 s time stands still for 1.8 s (`ancF` wrapper: 0, bosses .25), then foes move at .6 (bosses .8) until `END` 17; every foe takes x1.25 damage from everything (`hurt` wrapper) and towers attack x1.25 faster (`tStats` wrapper) while it is up. Shooting stars every 0.45 s on the six foes furthest along their road, preferring one not hit in the last 1.1 s: 220%, 100% within 0.5 tiles. Every 3.5 s (from 4.8 s) four stars link round the thickest crowd (a rotated square, 1.35 to the corners) and lash it: 500%, held 0.6 s (bosses 0.2) through `stunE`. At 17 s every star falls (`uFall`), big shooting stars rain on the foes, and at 18.05 s 1000% to every foe; the dome folds back into Sylph, who rises and fades by 21.5 s.
- **Looks.** Dome: a flattened hemisphere with a fresnel rim, meridians, parallels and round star specks (`domeM`). 1500 twinkling stars inside the dome (`starM`, bright ones with four spikes, size by `uScaleNow`), five constellations drawn star to star and turning with the sky (`NVR_CON`: a four-leaf clover, the spirit, her horn, a great star, the wind; `nvrRibbons`, drawn once), a nebula and a river of light (`nebM`, fbm), two arms of Sylph's wind spiralling in (`windM`), a star map on the ground at the rim (`aeonStrokes`). Sylph: a body of light, hair, a golden horn, four wings of wind and stars (`wingM`). Shooting stars: head, halo and a tapered tail (`trailM`), a four-spiked glint (`glintM`) where they land. Sounds: `nvrChime` bells, `sfxWind`.
- **Power.** Damage per second of cooldown with foes at 40x the hero's power in health (so the max-health parts weigh as in a late game): Never-Never Land 1.61, the Eldritch Tree 1.39, Abyssal Genesis 1.65 (the Aeon Dial, mythic, 1.58). It does not out-damage Abyssal Genesis; it pays with utility instead (time stop, the +25% to everything that hits, faster towers).
- Gotcha: the dome's star specks must be round (distance from the cell centre); a plain `floor()` cell lights up as a square that reads as grey specks on the board from the steep camera.
Tests: `tests/never-test.js` (27, in run-all). Shots: `tests/ancient-shots.js` (`__an.sheet(name,'never',[t...],{cam})`, load `tundra-shots.js` first).

## Artifact sets stay with their element, Never-Never Land turns violet (v362)
`_backup/v362_b.js`, inserted by `_backup/p_v362.py` (args: src html, module, version for the CLOG entry) before the boot line, on top of v361. Simon: "dark artifacts and the synergy of using 3 artifacts and its bonuses should ONLY applied to the scrolls of its corresponding element" and "for never-never land to turn purple, any dark spell needs to be cast inside it".
- **Sets.** Takes back the v356 dark-set change (`p_asdark.py`) that ran every hero scroll, and the statuses on foes it hit, in the dark set's colour context: the `castScrollNow` test is `SPELL_EL[k]===AS.el` again for every set, the `adkT` hurt stamp and the outer dark `statusTick` wrapper are gone (left as comments), and the dark clause of the inner 'on' test is cut. The ash and mist of a wither left by the hero's own dark scroll still take the purple (a dark context around that emit block in the update loop only). The Grave Runes card reads 'Your dark scrolls turn fully purple ...'. Individual artifacts were already element-gated (`artOnHit`/`artOnKill` test `el==='dark'` etc.).
- **Never-Never Land.** At the cast, a `castNever` wrapper finds the domain root (`scene.children[n0]`), gives every material in it a purple mix (`nvpMat`: shader materials get `uniform float uPur` and a last-line `mix(gl_FragColor.rgb,nvpMap(...),uPur)`, the rest an `onBeforeCompile` after `color_fragment`/`map_fragment`, cache key `|nvp`), wraps `root.add` so later children get it too, and stores the night light purple-mapped (`NVP.L0`/`LP`). Compiled in at cast time, so nothing recompiles later. A dark scroll cast by the hero while the domain is up (`castScrollNow` wrapper, before `NVR.END+1`) calls `nvpStart`: two violet shockwaves, a soft violet flash (scaled by `SPACE_K`), a chime and a toast; then the `update` wrapper eases `NVP.u` 0 to 1 over 1.2 s (smoothstep) and lerps `ANC.L.never` hc/hg/sc/bg; the domain's later 0xcfe6ff shockwaves are remapped. It resets when the domain ends. Same damage, same timing. The remap (`NVP_GL`/`nvpRGB`) is the dark set's: hues into the violet band, white to lavender, greys kept.
Tests: `tests/asdark-test.js` (19: other elements keep context 0 with the set on; dark scrolls draw purple with no leaks), `tests/never-test.js` (31: materials marked, a fire scroll does not trigger, a dark scroll turns it violet, back to 0 at the end). Sheet: `_backup/shots/nvp-violet.jpg`.

## Masteries take longer (v363)
Simon: "make all xp gain for damage dealing towers halfed and make it twice as hard to get mastery on a tower (70k -> 140k)". `_backup/mastx_b.js`, inserted before the boot line by `_backup/p_mastx.py` (args: src, module, version).
- **Kill XP halved:** `MAST_KILL` .5 multiplies the kill wrapper's grant, so a foe gives 0.5 XP, an elite 1.5, a boss 15 (the bell's kills too). The support buildings' `MAST_SUP` 4 XP per wave is unchanged: they deal no damage.
- **Levels doubled:** `MAST_XP` is now 2,000 / 8,000 / 24,000 / 60,000 / 140,000 (changed in place, so every reader and the developer Max button follow).
- **Nothing lost (Simon's choice):** `mastX2` doubles saved XP once per account (flag `meta.mastX2`), including developer mode's stash of the real progress (`meta.devSnap.mast`), so every building keeps its level and its progress. The save loaded at boot was migrated (line `let meta=migrateMeta(...)`) before the mastery code wrapped `migrateMeta`, so the module doubles the loaded meta directly and saves; imports and account switches go through the `migrateMeta` wrapper.
- The mastery card text says half an XP / 1.5 / 15.
- **Tests:** `tests/mastery-test.js` reads `MAST_KILL` and `MAST_XP` from the build (old builds still pass with 1 XP and the old levels) and checks the one-time doubling, including the developer stash.

## The Primordial Tempest, a true eye of the storm (v364)
Simon asked for faint translucent clouds swaying round the hero when the Tempest is cast, with the lightning circling the hero as if in high winds, so the hero is a true eye of the storm. Looks only: damage and timing are unchanged (tempest-test power still 7.1k one / 43k pack). `_backup/tpeye_b.js`, inserted before the boot line by `_backup/p_tpeye.py` (args: src, module, version; needs `castTempest`), which also hooks castTempest in five places (build after the pillar, step before `stepStrikes`, rain swirl, smoke swirl, `EYE.end()` at the end).
- **`tpEye(root,reg,regM,pool,hx,G0,hz,{CROWN,FIN,FALL,END},flashL)`** returns `{step(T,dt,Tm,P,fk,build), swirl(drop,WX,WZ), aura(P), end()}`; `TPE.on` holds the live eye (puffs, bands, arcs, streaks, `cw` wind, `env`, `api`) for tests.
- **Eye wall:** 96 cloud puffs (one `THREE.Points`, the 2x2 atlas `tpEyeTex()`, normal blending) in a band 1.25 to 3 tiles out, flaring with height, from the ground to about 3; 12 cloud bands (`tpRib` ribbons with a streaky noise shader) spiral in. Both wheel round counterclockwise seen from above (the same way as the ground cloud shadows), faster close in, sway in and out and up and down, are drawn in from far out as the storm gathers and are blown outward after the fall. Puffs on the camera side above 0.5 are thinned so the floating hero stays visible.
- **Wind:** `cw` spins up over 0-2.6, gusts at the crown bolt, rises 80% before the last thunder (`build`), eases after the fall. Rain drops within 6.5 tiles get a tangential wind (calm inside 1.2), the hero's smoke is emitted with a spin, dust and sparks are swept round over the ground (`pool`), pale wind streaks (30 ribbons) whip round, and `sfxWind` howls every 3.4 s.
- **Circling lightning:** up to 8 arcs (camera-facing ribbons, one draw) race round inside the wall at 1.3-2.65 tiles, re-jittered every 35 ms, lighting the puffs they pass (`aG`) and throwing sparks; a vBolt jumps between two clouds every 0.4-0.75 s (only while `fx` < 200).
- **Ribbons:** `tpRib(root,reg,regM,nS,nP,material,renderOrder)` keeps nS ribbons of nP points in one buffer; `put(s,P,W,AL)` takes flat points, `view(x,y,z)` sets one view direction per frame, `tpArcStep` walks a circle with a rotation step. The eye costs about 0.15 ms a frame (it was 1.2 ms with Vector3 ribbons and per-point trig).
- **Artifact sets:** colours created inside a set's context get `asOwn`, and every later `setHex`/`copy`/`lerp` re-maps them even inside `asOffRun`. The domain frame (`ancBegin`) makes its light colours inside that context, so under the lightning set the Tempest turned the board near-black. p_tpeye clears `asOwn` on the four light colours. The eye itself takes the set's colours (dark purple smoke and crimson arcs under Crimson Storm).
- **Tests:** `tests/tpeye-test.js` (33, in run-all): the wall, the clear eye, the direction, close-in speed, sway, gust, arcs, cloud bolts, streaks, bands, rain and smoke swirl, cost, ending, reset, the light under the lightning set, every land.

## Never-Never Land keeps its night under an artifact set (v365)
`_backup/v365_b.js`, inserted by `_backup/p_v365.py` (args: src html, module, version for the CLOG entry) before the boot line, on top of v362 or later. Found by the Primordial Tempest session (its v364 fixed the same thing for the Tempest under the lightning set): `ancBegin` makes the domain's light colours (`ANC.L[k].hc/hg/sc/bg`) inside the cast's set context, so they are born set-owned (`asOwn`) and the `THREE.Color` hooks re-map them on every later `setHex`/`copy`/`lerp`, even inside `asOffRun`. For Never-Never Land under the light set (Pale Verdict) the night went grey (ground light #0c1030 to #777886) and the board stayed day-lit under the dome; the v362 violet light (`NVP.L0`/`LP`, made in the same context) went grey too. Other sets don't touch its colours.
- A `castNever` wrapper (outermost) clears `asOwn` on the four colours and sets them from `NVP_NIGHT` inside `asOffRun`, then rebuilds `NVP.L0`/`LP` there. The dome, stars and Sylph still take the set's look; only the sky light is exempt.
- Notes: one more line in the v362 section; CLOG entry 'Never-Never Land keeps its night'.
Tests: `tests/never-test.js` (33: under all six sets the night keeps its colours; under the light set a dark cast still turns hc and hg violet). Sheets: `_backup/shots/nvlt-before.jpg` (v363 under the light set) and `nvlt-fixed.jpg`.
- Gotcha for any ancient domain: set `C.L` colours with `asOwn` cleared inside `asOffRun`, or a matching set re-maps the light.

## Scroll masteries (v366)
Simon: "add masteries to every spell ... common mastery 2 is like uncommon ... mastery 5 is like a rare ... mythic spells don't become domain type", and "reaching each mastery takes similar but different time ... rarer scrolls should level slower". `_backup/smast_b.js`, inserted before the boot line by `_backup/p_smast.py` (args: src, module, version), on top of v365. This release: the framework, the UI and the 11 Common scrolls; the other rarities follow, each in its own patch adding `SM_D`/`SMF`/`SMA`/`SMG` entries.
- **XP** (`meta.smast={scroll:xp}`, `DEV_KEYS.smast`): a `fireScroll` wrapper gives `cd x smRate(k)` per cast (`SM_RATE`: Common 1, each rarity 10% less, Ancient/Secret .53), only in a run (`state==='playing'`, not OW, not test casters). XP follows casting time, not kills, so rarer scrolls take longer. Thresholds are the buildings' `MAST_XP` (2k/8k/24k/60k/140k). `smLevelUp`: toast, flash on the caster.
- **Power ladder.** Measured 2026-10-25 over all 72 scrolls: a rarity tier is about 1.3-1.35x the one below in damage per second of cooldown (per cast it is about 2.2x, but cooldowns grow too). `SM_STEP=1.35`, `SM_X=[0,.5,1,1.35,1.75,2]` tiers per level (1 = halfway, 2 = next rarity, 5 = two up); Mythic/Ancient/Secret use `SM_XH` (half). `smTarget(k,L)`.
- **How a level reaches its target.** Effects first: each mastery is an add-on around the scroll's own cast. A `castScrollNow` wrapper makes a context per cast (`SM.ctx`, keyed by `o.smId`; `SMF[k].pre/cast`), a `hurt` wrapper runs `SMF[k].hit/kill` on the scroll's own hits (`F.dot` to include its dots) and applies marks (`e.smMk[el]`, '*' = all), and a `kill` wrapper runs `e.smDie` hooks. Mastery hits carry `o.smx` and never re-trigger. Then calibration (`tests/smast-cal.js`: 2 runs of 12 casts on 12 standing foes / 10 dying-and-replaced foes / 1 foe, weights .4/.35/.25): `SMA[k]` scales each mastery's numbers so a level never overshoots (solved level by level, so a mastery never weakens later), `SMG[k]` is the per-level damage factor that tops it up (never below 1; `fireScroll` multiplies P by `smP(k)`). Numbers in braces in the texts (`{20}`) are shown scaled by `SMA` (`smDesc`).
- **Kit:** `smShard` (element projectile), `smArc`, `smNova`, `smZone` (dps/slow/burn/ovg/enter), `smMark`, `smOnDie`, `smDot`, `smSlow`, `smStun` (bosses 40%), `smEcho` (the same scroll again at a share, `o.smx`), `smBehind`, `smOnLine`, `smRoadPt`, `smFreeze`, `smCastFx` (a ring of the element under the caster, brighter with the level). Objects it adds carry `userData.sm`.
- **UI:** a mastery bar on the scroll page (`renderSpellDetail` wrapper) opening the mastery card (`smOpen`, with "Power: like a Rare scroll" per level), pips on scroll cards (`renderSpells` wrapper) and battle buttons (`updScrollBar` wrapper), a dev-panel row (Max all / Reset, `smst5`/`smst0`).
- **The Commons:** Hexspark (Splinters, Hex Mark, Twin Spark, Black Nova, Hexstorm), Light Bolt (Radiant Trail, Dazzle, Prism Split, Sunpierce, Dawnbreaker), Basic Wither (Lingering Mist, Frailty, Contagion, Deep Rot, Blight Bloom), Ember Bolt (Kindling, Ember Spray, Flare, Scorched Ground, Twin Flames), Gravebind (Heavy Chains, Grave Drain, Linked Souls, Shackled, Mass Grave), Frost Shard (Splinters, Biting Cold, Brittle, Flash Freeze, Glacier Lance), Static Spark (Longer Arc, Overcharge, Static Shock, Discharge, Spark Orb), Cinder Scatter (More Cinders, Hot Coals, Smoke, Flashfire, Ember Carpet), Flame Lash (Long Lash, Searing, Backlash, Fire Snake, Inferno Cross), Thunderclap (Rolling Thunder, Stagger, Double Strike, Static Field, Thunderhead), Thorn Whip (Barbs, Double Lash, Snare, Briar Patch, Thorn Volley).
- **Gotchas:** keep `meta.smast` empty in the lab account (a leftover changes scroll damage in suites that cast through `fireScroll`). `SMK` is the smoke particle system, so the shape kit is `SMQ`. The tier ladder is front-loaded (Mastery 4 to 5 is only +8%), so Mastery 5 signatures must be modest in power.
Tests: `tests/smast-test.js` (21, in run-all): XP and rates, levels and the toast, the power factor, marks, on-death hooks, every Common at every mastery casting clean and tidying up, the ladder (Mastery 2 and 5 within 25% of target), the UI and the dev buttons.

## Mastery previews in developer mode (v367)
Simon: "when in dev mode when you hover over said mastery you see a preview ... like in clash royale ... this will allow me to tweak it much more easier". `_backup/smprev_b.js`, inserted before the boot line by `_backup/p_smprev.py` (args: src, module, version), on top of v366.
- **The preview page.** The first line of the main script (before the IIFE) keeps the page's original markup: `KK_SRCP={head, body (every body element before the main script), me (the main script element, whose text never changes)}`. `smPvSrc()` rebuilds the page from it as a `srcdoc` (same origin) with a first head script that sets `window.SMPREV=1` and turns `Storage.prototype.setItem/removeItem/clear` into no-ops, so the copy can never save over the account. Never write a literal script tag in module code: the patch strings split it ('scr'+'ipt').
- **Inside it** (`SMPV.mode`): sound off, every body element but `#stage` hidden. `__smPrev(k,L)` = `smPvStart`: plains, `buildMap(31)`, `newGame(1)`, no waves, `meta.spells={k}` (the hero carries nothing else), `meta.smast[k]=SM_XP[L-1]`. An `update` wrapper (`smPvTick`) keeps up to 7 foes walking from 3.2 tiles up the road past the hero (5x the hero's power in health, every 5th a knight with 9x, no gold), removes them 3 tiles beyond, and casts the scroll with `fireScroll` every clamp(cd, 2.2, 7) s (waiting while a domain is up). An `anim` wrapper holds the camera close over the hero and the road.
- **In the game:** in developer mode the mastery card's rows get `data-smpv` 1-5 and the scroll picture 0 (no mastery). Hover shows `#smPv` (next to the card on wide screens, above or below the row on narrow ones), moving off the rows hides it, a tap toggles it (not the tap a touch screen's own hover just opened). The frame is made on the first preview (a 1-2 s load, "Loading the preview"), reused while the card is open, and thrown away when the card closes (a MutationObserver).
- **Thresholds:** scroll masteries now read `SM_XP` (2k/8k/24k/60k/140k), kept apart from the buildings' `MAST_XP`.
Tests: `tests/smprev-test.js` (17, in run-all): the kept markup, the no-save preview page, the card in and out of developer mode, the panel, the arena logic run in the main page (foes, casts, camera, stop), the thresholds. The frame itself loads asynchronously and is checked by hand (a hidden browser pane does not animate it; step it with its own `__kk`).

## Building mastery previews in developer mode (v368)
Simon: "add this feature to also all building masteries". `_backup/bmprev_b.js`, inserted by `_backup/p_bmprev.py` (args: src, module, version), on top of v367. The building mastery card (`mastOpen` wrapper) gets the same hover/tap previews in developer mode (rows 1-5, the picture 0 = no mastery); `smPvShow(k,L,anchor,'tower')` and `smPvGo` call `__smPrevT` in the frame. There, `smPvStartT(type,L)` places the building on the free road-side tile nearest the middle of the board (`smPvSpot`) with `meta.mast[type]=MAST_XP[L-1]`; `smPvTickT` keeps 8 foes walking past (health 2.5 s of the building's fire, knights x2, no gold). Support buildings (`BMPV_AURA`: bell, windmill, merchant, mine, drum) get an archer beside them in the same row so the aura shows; paying ones (`BMPV_PAY`: merchant, mine, windmill) clear a wave every 7 s (`lmWaveClear`); the catapult and the trebuchet watch the road stretch 2.4 / 3 tiles away (`BMPV_WANT`). The camera pulls back a little for long range. `SMPV.kind` tells the two kinds apart.
Tests: `tests/bmprev-test.js` (9, in run-all): the card in and out of developer mode, the panel text, every building at Mastery 5 (starts, no errors, its foes get hit or it pays, the helper archer beside it, the long-range stretch), and scroll previews still working after.

## The Uncommon scrolls' masteries (v369)
Simon set the Uncommon ladder: "level one is just stronger uncommon, level 2 is approaching rare but not yet, level 3 is rare level power, level 4 is more than rare, level 5 is almost epic". `_backup/smast2_b.js`, inserted by `_backup/p_smast2.py` (args: src, module, version), on top of v368.
- **Ladders per rarity:** `SM_LAD.uncommon=[0,.3,.7,1,1.4,1.85]` (tiers above Uncommon); the v366 `smTarget` const is rewritten by the patch to read `SM_LAD` first (Commons keep `SM_X`). `smPowerLabel` says it in Simon's words for Uncommons. `SMW` gives a scroll its own calibration crowds (Wall of Fire: walking foes only; `tests/smast-cal.js` has a `walk` mode).
- **The masteries:** Dark Pulse (Dread, Echo Pulse, Shadow Tendrils, Undertow, Black Tide every 3rd pulse), Basic Beam (Searing Line, Prism Edge, Blinding, Lingering Light, Solar Lance every 3rd beam), Grasping Shadows (Long Grip, Crushing Grip, Outer Ring, Drag Under, Grave Hand), Soul Lantern (Brighter Wick, Haunting Light, Twin Lantern, Soul Harvest, Lantern of the Dead), Graveburst (Bone Shards, Open Grave, Deathmark, Aftershock, Mass Burial), Rime Bloom (Deep Chill, Ice Petals, Brittle, Frost Grip, Winter Rose), Ball Lightning (Arc Lash, Overcharge, Jolt, Twin Ball, Detonation), Sunflare (Corona, Solar Wind, Wider Light, Sunspots, Second Dawn), Catch Flame (Hotter Flame, Ember Burst, Wildfire, Ash Patch, Firestorm), Wall of Fire (Hotter Wall, Flare Up, Smoke Screen, Twin Wall, Wall of Ash), Bramble Patch (Sharper Thorns, Snagging Vines, Spreading Brambles, Thorn Burst, Rose Garden). Anchors follow each scroll's own cast: Dark Pulse on the hero, the beam along hero to target, Wall of Fire 1.5 tiles ahead of the lead foe, the rest on the densest foe.
- **Calibration:** Mastery 3 measured 1.27-1.46 (target 1.35, a Rare) and Mastery 5 1.51-1.89 (target 1.74, almost an Epic). Wall of Fire's damage is mostly its burn ticks, so its masteries add little and its factor (`SMG`) does most of the climb. One run of Dark Pulse (it pushes foes about), the Basic Beam (the line it takes) or the Wall of Fire (walking foes) swings by about 40%, so they were calibrated on 4-5 runs and the test only asks them to come out clearly stronger; the Beam's Solar Lance bursts on the thickest foes along the beam (its far end was often past them).
Tests: `tests/smast2-test.js` (9, in run-all); `tests/smast-test.js` now picks a scroll without masteries for its "come later" checks.

## The Rare scrolls' masteries (v370)
Simon: "finish masteries for rares, epic, legendaries and mythics. Don't touch ancients and secrets". Rare, Epic and Legendary reuse his Uncommon pattern (`SM_LAD.rare/epic/legendary` are copies of `SM_LAD.uncommon`: 1 a stronger one of its own rarity, 2 approaching the next, 3 the next rarity's power, 4 above it, 5 almost two up), with a general label (`smPowerLabel`). `_backup/smast3_b.js` + `_backup/p_smast3.py`, on top of v369.
- **The masteries:** Spatial Deflection (Second Fold), Riftfang (Gaping Rift, every 3rd cast), Lightning Cloud (Thunderstorm), Piercing Verdict (Radiant Verdict), Ice Tomb (Twin Tombs), Magma Geyser (Twin Geyser), Murder of Crows (Larger Murder, Raven King), Fire Tornado (Firestorm Collapse), Frost Wolves (Winter Howl), Entangling Roots (Sapling); new kit helpers `smStrong` (the toughest foe), `smSapling`.

- **Tests:** the rarity tests are generated: `python tests/gen-smast-test.py <rarity> <out> <version> <example> [noisy,ids] [left,out,ids]` writes `tests/smast3-test.js` etc. from `smast2-test.js`. Noisy scrolls are measured on 3 runs and only have to come out clearly stronger. Riftfang is noisy: its piercing shard lines up 1 or 5 of the standing test foes depending on their small random spread (one run gives 620 or 1220 a cast).

## The Epic and Legendary scrolls' masteries (v371 + v372, published together as v372)
`_backup/smast4_b.js` + `p_smast4.py` (v371: Spatial Loop, Piercing Judgment, Cataclysm, Phoenix, Frostfall Comet, Thunder Wyrm, Prism of Dawn, Oakheart Treant; helpers `smPillarL`, `smSingularity`, `smMiniComet`) and `_backup/smast5_b.js` + `p_smast5.py` (v372: Spatial Compression, Voidfall, Stormcaller, Chronostasis, Skyfall, Glacial Rift, Grim Harvest, Dimensional Slash, Hundred-Year Bloom; helpers `smVoidPillar`, `smGiantMeteor`).
- **Calibration:** next to a big scroll's own hit the add-ons are small (+4% to +35% raw at Mastery 5), so the factor `SMG` does most of the climb. Masteries never take power away, so a dip between two masteries in the measurements is read as noise (curve smoothed upward). The Spatial Loop deals no damage and keeps 1. Stormcaller, Grim Harvest, Dimensional Slash and the Piercing Judgment swing a lot run to run (random strikes, reaper aim, lines) and were measured on 6 runs.
- **Tests for Epic and up** measure like the calibration: the masteries' effects at base power, the multiplier on top. A big scroll's hits sit near the test foes' 600 HP, so a little more power tips a whole crowd from nearly dead to dead (Glacial Rift came out 2.4x with the factor applied inside, 1.8x linear), or a crowd it already wipes out cannot die faster (Chronostasis). Real waves have mixed health and scale smoothly.
- The clean-cast checks skip a cast when the crowd is already dead, as the game does (`big()` returns 'none'); the big scrolls kill the whole crowd at once and some casts throw on an empty list.
- `tests/smast-refine.js` (`__smr.F/fix`) measures with the factor applied and suggests SMG; kept as a tool.
Tests: `tests/smast4-test.js`, `tests/smast5-test.js` (9 each, in run-all; Stormcaller left out of the power check, like the Wall of Fire).
- **Set colours fix (v372):** an artifact set recolours its element's scrolls by casting them inside its colour context (`asRun`; `addFx` binds later effects to it). The mastery hooks wrapped `castScrollNow` from outside that context, so the cast flourish (`smCastFx`) and the add-ons (orbs, burning ground, rings) kept the scroll's own colours, which had been true since v366. `smast5_b.js` adds an outermost wrapper that runs the whole cast, hooks included, in the context. The run-all `sets` sweep caught it only because a calibration had left Piercing Judgment at Mastery 5 in the lab account. `tests/smsets-test.js` now casts all 31 mastered lightning/fire/ice/light scrolls at Mastery 5 with their set on (before the fix most leaked, after it none). Lesson: call `__smc.done()` after calibrating, or `meta.smast` stays in the lab account.

## The Mythic scrolls' masteries (v373)

The Mythics climb the half-step ladder (`SM_XH`: Mastery 5 = Ancient power, 1.35x). Every mastery adds a layer to the look, with a small effect beside it. Ancient and Secret scrolls have no masteries (Simon: "Don't touch ancients and secrets").
- **The masteries** (all replaced in v451-v458 by a set designed for each scroll; see those sections): Spatial Shatter (Glass Echo, Starlit Cracks, Falling Space, Prism Halo, Mirror World), Vael'Thoruun (Charged Sky, Thunder Crown, Static Field, Ball Lightning, Storm Lattice), Eclipse of the Damned (Umbral Mark, Black Corona, Grasping Dead, Dying Stars, Second Sunset), Wrath of the Ember Titan (White-Hot Blade, Ember Rain, Magma Fists, Molten Spray, Titan's Roar), Eternal Winter (Deep Cold, Snowfall, Ice Spires, Frostbite, Heart's Echo), Lance of the Seraph (Holy Brand, Feathers of Light, Radiant Wings, Eighth Lance, Choir of Lances), the Aeon Dial (Weathered, Sands of Ages, Clockwork Shards, Heavy Hours, The Thirteenth Hour), Wrath of Gaia (Thorned Bark, Pollen Storm, Blossoms, Strangling Roots, Heart of the Forest).
